# LEVEL 1 — CSS Fundamentals

---

## TOPIC: What is CSS?

### 1. 📌 Definition

#### What is it?
**What is CSS?** is a core styling concept within **LEVEL 1 — CSS Fundamentals** used in modern web engineering to define the visual layout, typography, colors, responsiveness, or animation behavior of web applications like Jibble.

#### In Simple Words
Think of **What is CSS?** as a specialized tool in a painter's toolbox that controls exactly how an element on the screen behaves, looks, or moves when rendered in a web browser.

---

### 2. 🎯 Why Do We Need It?

- **Problem it solves:** Eliminates ugly, unformatted HTML layouts and ensures components adapt seamlessly across mobile screens, tablets, and desktop displays.
- **Why CSS provides it:** Delivers standardized layout algorithms, GPU-accelerated animations, and responsive styling features.
- **When to use it:** Whenever styling, positioning, sizing, or animating UI components in web apps.
- **When NOT to use it:** Do not use CSS for backend server processing, data validation, or business logic.

---

### 3. 🧠 Core Concept

The core concept of **What is CSS?** centers on CSS parsing, DOM selection, and visual rendering via the browser layout engine.

```text
HTML Element Selection
   ↓
Apply Rule: [ What is CSS? ]
   ↓
Browser Layout / Composite Pipeline
   ↓
Rendered Visual UI Result
```

---

**px unit** is a core styling concept within **LEVEL 3 — Colors & Units** used in modern web engineering to define the visual layout, typography, colors, responsiveness, or animation behavior of web applications like Jibble.

#### In Simple Words
Think of **px unit** as a specialized tool in a painter's toolbox that controls exactly how an element on the screen behaves, looks, or moves when rendered in a web browser.

---

### 2. 🎯 Why Do We Need It?

- **Problem it solves:** Eliminates ugly, unformatted HTML layouts and ensures components adapt seamlessly across mobile screens, tablets, and desktop displays.
- **Why CSS provides it:** Delivers standardized layout algorithms, GPU-accelerated animations, and responsive styling features.
- **When to use it:** Whenever styling, positioning, sizing, or animating UI components in web apps.
- **When NOT to use it:** Do not use CSS for backend server processing, data validation, or business logic.

---

### 3. 🧠 Core Concept

The core concept of **px unit** centers on CSS parsing, DOM selection, and visual rendering via the browser layout engine.

```text
HTML Element Selection
   ↓
Apply Rule: [ px unit ]
   ↓
Browser Layout / Composite Pipeline
   ↓
Rendered Visual UI Result
```

**text-transform** is a core styling concept within **LEVEL 4 — Text & Fonts** used in modern web engineering to define the visual layout, typography, colors, responsiveness, or animation behavior of web applications like Jibble.

#### In Simple Words
Think of **text-transform** as a specialized tool in a painter's toolbox that controls exactly how an element on the screen behaves, looks, or moves when rendered in a web browser.

---

### 2. 🎯 Why Do We Need It?

- **Problem it solves:** Eliminates ugly, unformatted HTML layouts and ensures components adapt seamlessly across mobile screens, tablets, and desktop displays.
- **Why CSS provides it:** Delivers standardized layout algorithms, GPU-accelerated animations, and responsive styling features.
- **When to use it:** Whenever styling, positioning, sizing, or animating UI components in web apps.
- **When NOT to use it:** Do not use CSS for backend server processing, data validation, or business logic.

---

### 3. 🧠 Core Concept

The core concept of **text-transform** centers on CSS parsing, DOM selection, and visual rendering via the browser layout engine.

```text
HTML Element Selection
   ↓
Apply Rule: [ text-transform ]
   ↓
Browser Layout / Composite Pipeline
   ↓
Rendered Visual UI Result
```

**:focus pseudo-class** is a core styling concept within **LEVEL 11 — Pseudo-Classes & Pseudo-Elements** used in modern web engineering to define the visual layout, typography, colors, responsiveness, or animation behavior of web applications like Jibble.

#### In Simple Words
Think of **:focus pseudo-class** as a specialized tool in a painter's toolbox that controls exactly how an element on the screen behaves, looks, or moves when rendered in a web browser.

---

### 2. 🎯 Why Do We Need It?

- **Problem it solves:** Eliminates ugly, unformatted HTML layouts and ensures components adapt seamlessly across mobile screens, tablets, and desktop displays.
- **Why CSS provides it:** Delivers standardized layout algorithms, GPU-accelerated animations, and responsive styling features.
- **When to use it:** Whenever styling, positioning, sizing, or animating UI components in web apps.
- **When NOT to use it:** Do not use CSS for backend server processing, data validation, or business logic.

---

### 3. 🧠 Core Concept

The core concept of **:focus pseudo-class** centers on CSS parsing, DOM selection, and visual rendering via the browser layout engine.

```text
HTML Element Selection
```
